# AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.pbc_adic_cohomology_torsion_normal_form

## Reason

The normal-form statement and its new proof do not use any declaration from the
levelwise-truncation module. Its public import unnecessarily pulls in the full
normalized-constructible-effectivity and six-functor trace dependency chain. The
first delivery compiled all submitted files but was refused for three unused-simp
warnings in an unpublished `SfP1ChartExchange` dependency brought in only by this edge.
Remove the unused public import. The sole direct consumer, `AdicTorFlatCriterion`,
already explicitly publicly imports `LevelwiseAdicTruncations`, so its entire exported
context is preserved. No declaration, definition, instance, attribute or signature
changes, and no source-library file is edited.

## Old

```lean
public import Sheaf.Paper.LevelwiseAdicTruncations
```

## New

The import line is removed. All other existing public imports and every existing
public declaration signature remain verbatim. The new proof imports are private.

## Users

`tools/downstream.py Sheaf.Paper.AdicCohomologyTorsionNormalForm --uses
pbc_adic_cohomology_torsion_normal_form` reports only this item and
`Sheaf.Paper.AdicTorFlatCriterion` as textual users of the theorem. It lists the
following transitive importers; they are included for completeness. There is one
direct importer, `AdicTorFlatCriterion`, which has its own public import of the removed
re-export and is compiled unchanged against this delivery. Consequently the exported
context of every transitive importer is unchanged. No consumer copy is delivered.
Group entries reflect the local claim snapshot when this record was generated.
